```text
1	\section{Method}
2	\label{sec:method}
3	
4	\subsection{Problem Formulation}
5	\label{sec:formulation}
6	
7	A generation operator $G_\delta$ (LLM + decoding strategy $\delta$) produces successive generations $X \to Y_1 \to \cdots \to Y_K$, where $Y_k = G_\delta(Y_{k-1})$ and $Y_0 \triangleq X$ (Figure~\ref{fig:pipeline}).
8	This sequence forms a Markov chain.
9	By the Data Processing Inequality, $I(X; Y_1) \geq I(X; Y_2) \geq \cdots \geq 0$: the original signal degrades monotonically.
10	Together with Markov chain convergence, this motivates a finite $\Ktheory$ beyond which adjacent generations become indistinguishable; DPI guarantees existence but does not bound $\Ktheory$.
11	Specifically, DPI constrains the cumulative divergence from the seed text; the adjacent-pair convergence that determines $\Kempirical$ follows from the chain's approach to its stationary distribution, where successive transition distributions become indistinguishable.
12	The actual saturation depth must therefore be measured empirically.
13	
14	We define the operational lower bound $K^*_{\text{empirical}}$ (hereafter $\Kempirical$):
15	\begin{equation}
16	  \Kempirical = \max\!\bigl\{k \!\geq\! 1 : \mathrm{CI}_{\mathrm{low}}(\auc_{j\text{-}1,j}) > \theta,\ \forall j \!\leq\! k\bigr\},
17	  \label{eq:kstar}
18	\end{equation}
19	where $\mathrm{CI}_{\mathrm{low}}(\auc_{k-1,k})$ is the lower bound of a 95\% bootstrap confidence interval (1{,}000 resamples\footnote{We use 1{,}000 resamples as the default for computational efficiency; arXiv cross-scorer CIs (Table~\ref{tab:arxiv_crossscorer_ci}) use 10{,}000 to achieve tighter bounds at marginal boundaries where sub-pp differences determine the $\Kempirical{=}2$ vs.\ $\Kempirical{=}3$ verdict.}) over the pairwise AUC (Area Under the ROC Curve) between depths $k{-}1$ and $k$, and $\theta = 0.60$ is a post-hoc operational threshold (motivated below).
20	This criterion ensures that $\Kempirical$ reflects statistically robust discriminability rather than sampling noise; the $\forall j \leq k$ constraint is naturally satisfied because $\auc_{k,k+1}$ decreases monotonically for all pairs above $\theta$ across all tested configurations; negligible fluctuations ($\Delta < 0.02$) occur only at deeper depths where discrimination approaches chance level (Table~\ref{tab:kstar_ci}).
21	Since 0v1 $\auc > \theta$ in all tested configurations, $\Kempirical \geq 1$; $\Kempirical{=}0$ (undetectable first-generation text) is not observed.
22	$\Kempirical$ is an operational quantity jointly conditioned on the feature set and threshold $\theta$; the domain ordering is stable across $\theta \in [0.575, 0.65]$ (Section~\ref{sec:kstar_boundary}), though absolute $\Kempirical$ values shift with $\theta$.
23	We additionally verify that non-boundary-pair CI upper bounds fall below $\theta$ (Section~\ref{sec:kstar_boundary}).
24	
25	\paragraph{Threshold choice.}
26	We set $\theta{=}0.60$ as a post-hoc operational threshold, chosen at the empirical discontinuity in the AUC distribution (Figure~\ref{fig:kstar_theta}).
27	A 10{,}000-iteration permutation test yields a 95th-percentile null of ${<}\,0.55$ and $p < 0.002$ for all boundary comparisons, placing $\theta{=}0.60$ well above the empirical null.
28	$\Kempirical{=}2$ is stable across $\theta \in [0.55, 0.65]$ for all but two marginal configurations (Table~\ref{tab:kstar_ci_extended}): 1v2 AUC values cluster at $0.61$--$0.70$ while 2v3 values cluster at $0.55$--$0.59$, creating a ${\sim}$5--11\,pp discontinuity.
29	Only at extremes does $\Kempirical$ shift: $\theta{=}0.55$ yields $\Kempirical{=}3$ for two Qwen configurations; $\theta{=}0.70$ yields $\Kempirical{=}1$ across all configurations.
30	The downstream filtering evaluation (Section~\ref{sec:filtering}) uses the OBD classifier without re-optimizing $\theta$, avoiding circularity.
31	A leave-one-family-out (LOFO) cross-validation on C4 provides an internal consistency check (not external validation): each of five folds derives $\hat{\theta}$ from the gap midpoint between $\min(\auc_{1v2})$ and $\max(\auc_{2v3})$ over the remaining families, yielding $\hat{\theta} \in [0.5725, 0.6004]$; $\theta{=}0.60$ falls within this range, and 8/9 held-out predictions match the original $\Kempirical$ (the single mismatch is the borderline rewrite-mode configuration; Table~\ref{tab:theta_lofo}).
32	ArXiv corroborates with 6/6 LOFO agreement; Wikipedia is underdetermined because most models produce near-chance 1v2 AUC, invalidating the gap-based derivation (Appendix~\ref{sec:theta_lofo}).
33	
34	
35	\subsection{Features and Classifier}
36	\label{sec:features}
37	
38	Each document is characterized by a 15-dimensional feature vector spanning four groups: \emph{surprisal moments} (mean, std, skewness, kurtosis; 4~dims), \emph{surprisal derivatives} (mean and std of first- and second-order differences, plus first-order skewness; 5~dims), \emph{vocabulary diversity} (type-token ratio, hapax legomena ratio, self-BLEU; 3~dims), and \emph{frequency tail statistics} (frequency kurtosis, entropy, rare token fraction; 3~dims).
39	All features are computed from token-level surprisal values via an external scorer model (full definitions in Table~\ref{tab:s5_features}, Appendix).
40	Self-BLEU is computed within each CV fold using only same-fold, same-depth reference samples to prevent leakage; it ranks \#1 in 1/8 conditions (greedy decoding) but is absent from the top-3 in all 7 others.
41	Removing self-BLEU reduces mean $\auc_{1v2}$ by $< 1$\,pp across all models (14D $\Kempirical = 2$ for all 4 configurations; bootstrap CIs in Table~\ref{tab:selfbleu_ablation}); in deployment where depth labels are unavailable, self-BLEU can be replaced with corpus-level self-similarity or omitted with minimal performance loss.
42	Hand-crafted features enable cross-generator transfer without retraining and permit attribution of discriminative power to specific text properties.
43	
44	We use \emph{Ordinal Binary Decomposition} (OBD), decomposing the $(K{+}1)$-class problem into $K$ cumulative binary sub-classifiers, each estimating $P(\text{depth} > k \mid \mathbf{x})$ via LightGBM~\cite{ke2017lightgbm} (hyperparameters in Table~\ref{tab:s6_lgbm_params}).
45	OBD achieves lower MAE than multiclass LightGBM (e.g., 0.984 vs.\ 1.016 on Qwen-rewrite) while maintaining superior pairwise $\auc$ (mean 0.6095 vs.\ 0.4883 for ordinal logistic regression).
46	
47	\subsection{Experimental Setup}
48	\label{sec:experimental_setup}
49	
50	We evaluate ten configurations spanning six architecture families (Table~\ref{tab:setup}): Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct (rewrite and continuation modes), Qwen2.5-1.5B (base, continuation), Qwen2.5-7B, Qwen2.5-14B, Pythia-1.4B, OLMo-1B, Gemma-2-2B, LLaMA-3.1-8B, and Mistral-7B (all continuation except Qwen-rw).
51	Each uses 5{,}000 C4~\cite{raffel2020t5} seeds truncated to 256 tokens, recursively generated for $K{=}5$ iterations (30{,}000 documents per configuration).
52	Default decoding: nucleus $p{=}0.95$, $T{=}1.0$; decoding ablation covers greedy, $T{=}0.7$, and $T{=}1.2$.
53	Continuation mode truncates seeds to 128 tokens and extends to 256; rewrite mode paraphrases the full text.
54	Evaluation: 5-fold GroupKFold cross-validation grouped by seed document; pairwise $\auc$ as primary metric.
55	
56	\begin{table}[t]
57	\centering
58	\small
59	\caption{Experimental configurations for depth-chain generation. \textbf{Config}: shorthand identifier; \textbf{Model}: architecture and parameter count; \textbf{Mode}: generation strategy (continuation or rewrite); $N$: number of seed documents per depth level.}
60	\label{tab:setup}
61	\resizebox{\columnwidth}{!}{%
62	\begin{tabular}{llcc}
63	\toprule
64	\textbf{Config} & \textbf{Model} & \textbf{Mode} & $N$ \\
65	\midrule
66	Qwen-rw   & Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct & Rewrite & 5{,}000 \\
67	Qwen-cont & Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\
68	Qwen-base & Qwen2.5-1.5B          & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\
69	Pythia    & Pythia-1.4B            & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\
70	OLMo      & OLMo-1B               & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\
71	Gemma     & Gemma-2-2B            & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\
72	Qwen-7B   & Qwen2.5-7B            & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\
73	Qwen-14B  & Qwen2.5-14B           & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\
74	LLaMA-8B  & LLaMA-3.1-8B          & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\
75	Mistral   & Mistral-7B            & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\
76	\bottomrule
77	\end{tabular}}
78	\end{table}
79

$\\Kempirical{=}0$ (undetectable first-generation text) is not observed.\n$\\Kempirical$ is an operational quantity jointly conditioned on the feature set and threshold $\\theta$; the domain ordering is stable across $\\theta \\in [0.575, 0.65]$ (Section~\\ref{sec:kstar_boundary}), though absolute $\\Kempirical$ values shift with $\\theta$.\nWe additionally verify that non-boundary-pair CI upper bounds fall below $\\theta$ (Section~\\ref{sec:kstar_boundary}).\n\n\\paragraph{Threshold choice.}\nWe set $\\theta{=}0.60$ as a post-hoc operational threshold, chosen at the empirical discontinuity in the AUC distribution (Figure~\\ref{fig:kstar_theta}).\nA 10{,}000-iteration permutation test yields a 95th-percentile null of ${<}\\,0.55$ and $p < 0.002$ for all boundary comparisons, placing $\\theta{=}0.60$ well above the empirical null.\n$\\Kempirical{=}2$ is stable across $\\theta \\in [0.55, 0.65]$ for all but two marginal configurations (Table~\\ref{tab:kstar_ci_extended}): 1v2 AUC values cluster at $0.61$--$0.70$ while 2v3 values cluster at $0.55$--$0.59$, creating a ${\\sim}$5--11\\,pp discontinuity.\nOnly at extremes does $\\Kempirical$ shift: $\\theta{=}0.55$ yields $\\Kempirical{=}3$ for two Qwen configurations; $\\theta{=}0.70$ yields $\\Kempirical{=}1$ across all configurations.\nThe downstream filtering evaluation (Section~\\ref{sec:filtering}) uses the OBD classifier without re-optimizing $\\theta$, avoiding circularity.\nA leave-one-family-out (LOFO) cross-validation on C4 provides an internal consistency check (not external validation): each of five folds derives $\\hat{\\theta}$ from the gap midpoint between $\\min(\\auc_{1v2})$ and $\\max(\\auc_{2v3})$ over the remaining families, yielding $\\hat{\\theta} \\in [0.5725, 0.6004]$; $\\theta{=}0.60$ falls within this range, and 8/9 held-out predictions match the original $\\Kempirical$ (the single mismatch is the borderline rewrite-mode configuration; Table~\\ref{tab:theta_lofo}).\nArXiv corroborates with 6/6 LOFO agreement; Wikipedia is underdetermined because most models produce near-chance 1v2 AUC, invalidating the gap-based derivation (Appendix~\\ref{sec:theta_lofo}).\n\n\n\\subsection{Features and Classifier}\n\\label{sec:features}\n\nEach document is characterized by a 15-dimensional feature vector spanning four groups: \\emph{surprisal moments} (mean, std, skewness, kurtosis; 4~dims), \\emph{surprisal derivatives} (mean and std of first- and second-order differences, plus first-order skewness; 5~dims), \\emph{vocabulary diversity} (type-token ratio, hapax legomena ratio, self-BLEU; 3~dims), and \\emph{frequency tail statistics} (frequency kurtosis, entropy, rare token fraction; 3~dims).\nAll features are computed from token-level surprisal values via an external scorer model (full definitions in Table~\\ref{tab:s5_features}, Appendix).\nSelf-BLEU is computed within each CV fold using only same-fold, same-depth reference samples to prevent leakage; it ranks \\#1 in 1/8 conditions (greedy decoding) but is absent from the top-3 in all 7 others.\nRemoving self-BLEU reduces mean $\\auc_{1v2}$ by $< 1$\\,pp across all models (14D $\\Kempirical = 2$ for all 4 configurations; bootstrap CIs in Table~\\ref{tab:selfbleu_ablation}); in deployment where depth labels are unavailable, self-BLEU can be replaced with corpus-level self-similarity or omitted with minimal performance loss.\nHand-crafted features enable cross-generator transfer without retraining and permit attribution of discriminative power to specific text properties.\n\nWe use \\emph{Ordinal Binary Decomposition} (OBD), decomposing the $(K{+}1)$-class problem into $K$ cumulative binary sub-classifiers, each estimating $P(\\text{depth} > k \\mid \\mathbf{x})$ via LightGBM~\\cite{ke2017lightgbm} (hyperparameters in Table~\\ref{tab:s6_lgbm_params}).\nOBD achieves lower MAE than multiclass LightGBM (e.g., 0.984 vs.\\ 1.016 on Qwen-rewrite) while maintaining superior pairwise $\\auc$ (mean 0.6095 vs.\\ 0.4883 for ordinal logistic regression).\n\n\\subsection{Experimental Setup}\n\\label{sec:experimental_setup}\n\nWe evaluate ten configurations spanning six architecture families (Table~\\ref{tab:setup}): Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct (rewrite and continuation modes), Qwen2.5-1.5B (base, continuation), Qwen2.5-7B, Qwen2.5-14B, Pythia-1.4B, OLMo-1B, Gemma-2-2B, LLaMA-3.1-8B, and Mistral-7B (all continuation except Qwen-rw).\nEach uses 5{,}000 C4~\\cite{raffel2020t5} seeds truncated to 256 tokens, recursively generated for $K{=}5$ iterations (30{,}000 documents per configuration).\nDefault decoding: nucleus $p{=}0.95$, $T{=}1.0$; decoding ablation covers greedy, $T{=}0.7$, and $T{=}1.2$.\nContinuation mode truncates seeds to 128 tokens and extends to 256; rewrite mode paraphrases the full text.\nEvaluation: 5-fold GroupKFold cross-validation grouped by seed document; pairwise $\\auc$ as primary metric.\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{Experimental configurations for depth-chain generation. \\textbf{Config}: shorthand identifier; \\textbf{Model}: architecture and parameter count; \\textbf{Mode}: generation strategy (continuation or rewrite); $N$: number of seed documents per depth level.}\n\\label{tab:setup}\n\\resizebox{\\columnwidth}{!}{%\n\\begin{tabular}{llcc}\n\\toprule\n\\textbf{Config} & \\textbf{Model} & \\textbf{Mode} & $N$ \\\\\n\\midrule\nQwen-rw   & Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct & Rewrite & 5{,}000 \\\\\nQwen-cont & Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\\\\nQwen-base & Qwen2.5-1.5B          & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\\\\nPythia    & Pythia-1.4B            & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\\\\nOLMo      & OLMo-1B               & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\\\\nGemma     & Gemma-2-2B            & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\\\\nQwen-7B   & Qwen2.5-7B            & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\\\\nQwen-14B  & Qwen2.5-14B           & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\\\\nLLaMA-8B  & LLaMA-3.1-8B          & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\\\\nMistral   & Mistral-7B            & Continuation & 5{,}000 \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}}\n\\end{table}\n",
